| REG-28 | 404 chrome drift (NF-01) — the top-level `[...rest]` catch-all hard-404 renders Next's bare `__next_error__` shell (no `<html lang/dir>`, no chrome, generic title), contradicting BL-01-FIX's 2026-07-02 runtime-verified locale-aware/chrome-preserving 404 claim (likely an intervening-track side effect; `[...rest]` is a sibling of `(public)`, so unrelated to T04's own `loading.tsx` fix) | T04 SECOND FINDING | Dedicated 404-chrome pass | ✅ Closed by PRE-GATE-FIX (2026-07-19, Opus 4.8). **ROOT CAUSE:** the app's only `<html>`/`<body>` live in the dynamic `[locale]/layout.tsx` (there is no `app/layout.tsx`). On next@15.5 a `notFound()` that bubbles out of that dynamic-root segment renders on the special `/_not-found` route which sits ABOVE `[locale]`, so the layout's `<html lang/dir>` never re-applies → the bare `__next_error__` shell (vercel/next.js#64870, "still the case in 15.5"). This was a standing framework limitation, NOT a change introduced by a specific commit — BL-01-FIX's runtime claim predates the drift becoming visible. **FIX:** enabled `experimental.globalNotFound` + added `src/app/global-not-found.tsx` (full localized HTML document — reads negotiated locale from the `NEXT_LOCALE` cookie the middleware sets, `<html lang/dir>` + NextIntlClientProvider + kit `EmptyState`). Removed the `[locale]/[...rest]` catch-all so unknown paths are genuinely unmatched → routed to `global-not-found` (verified: `/xx`,`/does-not-exist`,`/en/does-not-exist`,`/xx/admin` → hard 404 + `noindex` + correct localized full-doc shell). `[locale]/not-found.tsx` kept (now bilingual via next-intl, was hardcoded-Arabic) for explicit `notFound()` from matched detail pages. **RESIDUAL (accepted):** explicit `notFound()` from a MATCHED route (listing/category/store missing record) still renders localized EmptyState CONTENT inside Next's default shell (no `lang/dir` wrapper) — the same #64870 limitation, unfixable without abandoning the unprefixed-default-locale architecture; status stays hard 404 + `noindex`. |

| REG-33 | R-S03 slug change-once is enforced by the APP LAYER ONLY — live `betk.stores` has NO trigger/CHECK/constraint governing the change (only `chk_store_slug_fmt` / `uq_stores_slug` / `uq_stores_seller`, live-verified via pg_constraint + pg_trigger). `updateStoreProfile` (T06) is the sole guard: it rejects a changed slug when `slug_changed_at IS NOT NULL` and additionally scopes the slug UPDATE with `slug_changed_at IS NULL` (race-safe). A malicious direct client bypassing the action could, in principle, UPDATE its own store's slug twice (stores_manage RLS permits own-row UPDATE and does not inspect slug_changed_at) | Minted by Phase 04 / T06 (2026-07-20, Sonnet) | **Candidate hardening** — a `BEFORE UPDATE` trigger on `betk.stores` raising when `NEW.slug <> OLD.slug AND OLD.slug_changed_at IS NOT NULL` would make R-S03 DB-authoritative. No DB object added in T06 (task-scoped app-layer). **WHY it matters (T08): today `updateStoreProfile` is the ONLY writer of `stores.slug`, so the app guard is sufficient in practice — but future multi-writer paths break that assumption. Specifically a Phase-14 admin edit/backfill (or any second server-side writer that touches `stores`) would bypass an app-only guard and could change a slug twice, since `stores_manage` RLS permits own-row UPDATE and does not inspect `slug_changed_at`. | REG-38 | Locale-switch UX: `AppChrome`/`LanguageSwitcher`'s `router.replace(pathname,{locale})` is a full server round-trip (next-intl remounts the whole `[locale]` subtree) with no visible pending state, compounded by NO `(public)` route-level `loading.tsx` (forbidden by the hard-404 binding rule) — the locale toggle "feels dead" and in-page navigation "feels stuck" | DIAG-PERF-01 A1+A2/DIAG-01+02 (2026-07-20, Opus 4.8) | Two legs: (1) app-layer pending-state on the toggle itself; (2) `(public)` route-level skeleton-streaming strategy — Claude Design (UI Spec §6 / Brief §5.24) | **Leg (1) ✅ CLOSED by PERF-01 (2026-07-21, Sonnet, `feature/perf-01`)** — both locale toggles (`AppChrome` + `account/LanguageSwitcher`) wrap `router.replace` in `useTransition`; `LanguageSwitcher`'s plain `<select>` (app-layer, already unstyled) gets `disabled`+`aria-busy`+token `opacity-60`/`cursor-progress` directly; `AppChrome`'s wrapper div gets `aria-busy`+`opacity-60`+`pointer-events-none` while pending (its own click-handler also no-ops while pending) — **STOP-and-flag, CD-DELTA-4 candidate:** `AppTopbar`'s language `<button>` (frozen `components/shared`) has no `disabled`/`className` passthrough, so the button itself can't carry the pending affordance directly; code-commented in `AppChrome.tsx`. **Leg (2) stays Open (owned, Claude Design)** — no route-level `(public)` `loading.tsx`/skeleton strategy added (binding rule holds; still a holistic Design decision). PERF-01 additionally shipped a narrower, complementary mitigation on 2 specific `(public)` routes — component-level `<Suspense>` streaming (not a route `loading.tsx`) on `/category/[slug]` (`CategoryListingsSection`) and `/store/[slug]`'s Listings tab (`StoreListingsSection`), both `SkeletonGrid`-fallback, homepage-precedent — so header/chrome paints immediately while just the listings query streams in; `/search` and `/listing/[id]` are untouched (out of PERF-01's STEP 1 scope) and still have no per-route feedback, so leg (2)'s general strategy decision remains open. **Noted at HOUSEKEEP-P5 (2026-07-21): leg (2) is pending CD-DELTA-4 (Claude Design — requested, in flight).** ✅ **CLOSED by CD-DELTA-4 LAND (2026-07-21, Sonnet, `feature/phase-05-listings`)** — leg (1)'s AppTopbar API gap is filled: the shared `AppTopbar` gained the additive `langPending` prop (disabled + `aria-busy` + token `opacity-60`/`cursor-progress` ON THE BUTTON, zero visual change unset — the only sanctioned edit point, every other prop/JSX byte-preserved, diff-verified vs main); `AppChrome` now passes `langPending={isLocalePending}` and the PERF-01 wrapper workaround (the `pointer-events-none`/`opacity-60` chrome `<div>`) was REMOVED since the button fully expresses pending; `account/LanguageSwitcher` unchanged. Leg (2) landed as the token-only shared `RouteProgress` top bar (REG-38b ADOPTED — `fixed inset-x-0 top-0`, `z-60`, indeterminate `--primary` segment, renders null at rest) mounted in BOTH the public/buyer `AppChrome` and the seller `SellerChrome` shells (driven by a `useTransition` route-transition state), plus 3 additive route-transition skeletons (`CategoryPageSkeleton`/`ListingDetailSkeleton`/`StorefrontSkeleton`) on `CatalogSkeletons` (additive exports only, nothing renamed/removed). Brief §5.12/§5.13/§5.24 addenda applied. |
| REG-39 | 8 seeded category `icon_url` paths (`/icons/categories/{clothing,food,home,beauty,jewelry,arts,books,services}.svg`) all 404 in production (evidence: Vercel prod logs, 2026-07-20) — the icon assets were referenced in seed data but never produced/deployed to that path | Minted by PERF-01 (2026-07-21, Sonnet) | Real assets = **CD-DELTA-4** (Claude Design). PERF-01 ships a feature-layer stopgap only (drop the known-missing URLs at the mapping so the shared `CategoryGrid`'s Lucide default renders). | **Stopgap ✅ CLOSED by PERF-01 (2026-07-21, Sonnet, `feature/perf-01`)** — `HomeCategoryGrid.tsx` (feature layer, NOT `components/shared` — the `<img>` was already composed here) now drops any `iconUrl` under the known-missing `/icons/categories/` prefix at the mapping (`resolveIconUrl`), so `icon` resolves to `undefined` and the shared `CategoryGrid` renders its own Lucide `<Tag>` default; code-commented `REG-39, real assets via CD-DELTA-4`. Runtime-verified on the homepage: 0 actual `<img>` tags reference `/icons/categories/*` (zero browser-fetchable requests — the raw string still appears once, harmlessly, inside the RSC hydration payload's serialized props JSON, which is not a fetch target), 8/8 top-level categories render the `lucide-tag` fallback glyph instead. **Real-assets leg stays Open (owned, CD-DELTA-4).** **Noted at HOUSEKEEP-P5 (2026-07-21): CD-DELTA-4 has been requested and is in flight (not yet delivered).** ✅ **CLOSED by CD-DELTA-4 LAND (2026-07-21, Sonnet, `feature/phase-05-listings`)** — the 8 theme-neutral single-color (fixed brand teal `#1c8d7e`, sanctioned asset literal — CSS vars can't reach inside an `<img>`) 24×24 outline glyphs now ship at their exact seeded paths `public/icons/categories/{clothing,food,home,beauty,jewelry,arts,books,services}.svg` (byte-diff-verified vs the handoff templates). Runtime-verified on `next start`: the homepage HTML references all 8 `/icons/categories/*.svg`, each returns HTTP 200 (`image/svg+xml`), and there are ZERO `lucide-tag` fallbacks for the 8 top-level categories. Both legs now closed. |
| REG-40 | next-intl + on-demand ISR locale rule (STANDING, load-bearing): on an ISR route under `[locale]` whose `generateStaticParams` returns `[]` (nothing prebuilt → every path generated on demand at first hit / revalidation), next-intl's `setRequestLocale` `cache()` store is NOT reliably resolved across the independently-rendered layout / page / Suspense-child scopes for the NON-default locale — a store-based `getLocale()`/`getTranslations()` (or server `<Link>`, which calls `getLocale()` internally) then falls back to `headers()` → static generation aborts with `DYNAMIC_SERVER_USAGE` → **500 on `/en/…` while `/…` (AR) is 200** (the default locale is silently masked by next-intl's fallback). Build-time-prerendered routes (homepage, `/en/search` shell, authed shells) mask it — the failure only shows on the on-demand / revalidation path. | Discovered + fixed by PERF-02 (2026-07-21, Opus, `feature/perf-01`) during the STEP-4 runtime smoke | ✅ **Fix applied + STANDING RULE recorded** (`CACHING_STRATEGY.md §7`): thread `locale` EXPLICITLY on every ISR render path — `getTranslations({ locale, namespace })` everywhere (incl. the shared `(public)/layout.tsx`, which wraps all public pages), `locale` as a prop into Suspense-wrapped async children (`CategoryListingsSection`), and `locale={locale}` on server `<Link>` (`SubcategoryChips` + the category empty-state links). `setRequestLocale` is still called (primes the client provider) but the render path no longer depends on it. Any future ISR route under `[locale]` MUST follow this (verify: `next start`, hit `/en/<route>` first+repeat — a 500 on `/en` while `/` is 200 is this bug). | Standing (rule) — closed for category/listing; applies to all future ISR routes |

## Phase 06 entry checklist (carry-forwards into Messaging & Inquiries — schedule into owning tasks, don't lose them)
> Pinned 2026-07-22 (Phase 05 / T06 exit gate). **Phase 06 = Messaging & Inquiries** (`BETK_PHASES.md` Phase 06 = FR-BUY-5 + FR-SEL-13: inquiries + inquiry_messages threads, confirm→checkout enablement, avg_response_hours, notify ≤5s R-N04). These are first-class tasks of the owning Phase-06 build, not surprises.
- **`inquiry_messages` owner-write policies (ERD §3 map row 8 — Messaging phase).** Live pg_policies-verified at T06: `inquiries` carries ONLY `inq_buyer` (SELECT); the seller-side inquiry SELECT + ALL `inquiry_messages` policies are **ABSENT** (RLS-enabled, zero-policy → default-deny), exactly the open-issue-#14 / REG-29 / REG-31 / REG-34 class. Phase 06 MUST mint the ERD §3-specced policies (thread participants read+write their own inquiry's messages; scope via the parent `inquiries` buyer_id/store owner) as a first-class T0x task — additive migration per REG-24 + the REG-32 types procedure if any rpc lands. **Confirm the exact `inquiries` participant SELECT + `inquiry_messages` shape against `BETK_ERD.md §3` before coding; STOP-and-flag if unpinned.** Likely mints the NEXT REG-# in this class (REG-41-class candidate — do not pre-reserve elsewhere).
- **confirm→checkout enablement contract (feeds Phase 07).** A seller-confirmed inquiry is what UNLOCKS checkout (AC-BUY-6: an order can only be created from a confirmed inquiry). Phase 06 owns writing the confirm transition + the flag/state Phase 07 checkout reads; pin the contract (which column/state on `inquiries` marks "confirmed → checkout-enabled") so Phase 07's atomic order-create can gate on it. Cite the ERD/schema field, don't invent.
- **`avg_response_hours` mechanism — UNPINNED (cite-or-flag, REG-26-class).** `seller_profiles.avg_response_hours` (numeric(5,2)) EXISTS in the schema but live-verified has **NO trigger/function** maintaining it (zero user-defined triggers on `inquiries`/`inquiry_messages`; no `%response%` trigger) — same "column exists, mechanism unpinned" shape as REG-26 (`view_count`). Phase 06 must DECIDE: app-layer recompute on seller reply (update the metric in the message-send action) OR formally defer + flag (mint a REG-# and render the static/NULL value). Do NOT improvise a service-role reach-around.
- **R-N04 notify ≤5s depends on the notifications infra (Phase 12).** "Notify the other party within 5s of a new inquiry/message" (R-N04) rides the notifications system, which is Phase-12-owned (`notifications` writes + channel prefs + `restock_alerts` all deferred there per the ERD §3 map). Phase 06 should wire the message-send EVENT but flag the actual delivery (email/WhatsApp/in-app) as a Phase-12 dependency rather than building a bespoke notify path — same deferral discipline as Phase 05's "buyers waiting" count.

## ERD §3 specced-but-absent-policy tables — owning-phase map (PINNED, 13 tables)
> Pinned 2026-07-19 (Phase 03 T07 gate) from the REG-25-FIX live `pg_policies` vs `BETK_ERD.md §3` audit. These tables are RLS-**enabled** but ship with **ZERO policies today** (the Phase-01 T08-F1 "policies arrive in the owning phase" class → default-deny for all until then). This is **not a defect** — each row's policies are owned by the phase that first needs the table's writes. Future phase-entry checklists MUST inherit their row: adding the specced policy is a first-class task of that phase, not a surprise. No policy here is Phase 03's to add (Phase 03 is read-only public + the two already-live self-scope write policies).

| # | Table(s) | Owning phase / decision | Note |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1–3 | `order_items`, `order_status_history`, `order_messages` | **Phase 07 — checkout/orders** (order set) | Policies arrive with order creation/confirmation; `order_items` referenced historically by soft-deleted listings (R-L10). `order_messages` write path may co-land with messaging — see #10. |
| 4–5 | `shipments`, `shipment_tracking_events` | **Phase 07 — orders/delivery** | Seller-scoped write + buyer read on their own order's shipments. |
| 6–7 | `dispute_evidence`, `dispute_messages` | **Disputes phase** | `disputes` itself already has its SELECT policy; the evidence/message children are owed by the disputes build. |
| 8 | `inquiry_messages` | **Messaging phase** | `inquiries` parent already policied; message rows owed by messaging. |
| 9 | `restock_alerts` | **Notifications phase** | Backs R-N06 sold_out restock CTA (the CTA renders in Phase 03; the subscribe/write path + policy are notifications' to build). |
| 10 | `seller_strikes` | **Admin phase** | Admin-write / seller-read own. |
| 11 | `flagged_content` | **Admin phase** | Admin moderation surface. |
| 12 | `whatsapp_templates` | **Admin phase** | Merged tab under Admin → Settings → Notifications (OD-5); not a standalone route. |
| 13 | `sessions` | **Intentionally UNUSED (ADR-010)** | No policy owed — Supabase Auth owns session state; `betk.sessions` is a legacy/unused artifact per ADR-010, not a live surface. |

## Schema deltas to apply in Phase 01 migration (vs C3 baseline)
- migration 003: `CREATE TYPE auth_provider AS ENUM ('phone','google');`
- migration 004 (users): `phone_number` nullable; add `auth_provider NOT NULL DEFAULT 'phone'`, `deleted_at TIMESTAMPTZ NULL`, `anonymized_at TIMESTAMPTZ NULL`.
- Transaction gate: Server Action + RLS WITH CHECK requiring `phone_number IS NOT NULL` on orders/seller_profiles/payouts inserts.
- R-A05 login block also checks `deleted_at IS NULL`.

## Execution model
- Skills auto-attach via `.cursor/rules/*.mdc` (core always-on + glob-scoped DB/actions/ui/tests + agent-requested security/UI reviews). You pick the model only; the core rule flags a model mismatch. Ref: HOW_RULES_AUTORUN.md.

Binding rules held (no `loading.tsx` wrapping `notFound()`-capable segments; zero `components/ui/*`/`shared/*` internal edits). **REG-28 — INVESTIGATE:** confirmed the drift by probing prod (`next start`): every unknown route returned the bare `<html id="__next_error__">` shell (no `lang/dir`, no NextIntl, generic title). ROOT CAUSE (not a specific-commit regression): the app's ONLY `<html>`/`<body>` live in the DYNAMIC `[locale]/layout.tsx` (there is no `app/layout.tsx`), so a `notFound()` bubbling out of that segment renders on the special `/_not-found` route which sits ABOVE `[locale]` → the layout's document shell never re-applies (vercel/next.js#64870, confirmed "still the case in 15.5"). Empirically re-proved: removing `[locale]/not-found.tsx` did NOT help (bubbled `notFound()` still bare); the fix had to be at the routing level. **REG-28 — RESTORE:** enabled `experimental.globalNotFound` in `next.config.ts` + added `src/app/global-not-found.tsx` (a FULL localized HTML document — reads the negotiated locale from the `NEXT_LOCALE` cookie the middleware sets, validated vs `routing.locales`, fallback `ar`; renders `<html lang/dir>` + `NextIntlClientProvider` + kit `EmptyState` + locale-aware home link; kit components only, no new 404 design). Added a `notFound.*` namespace to BOTH catalogs. **Removed the `[locale]/[...rest]` catch-all** — it was MATCHING every unknown path then throwing `notFound()`, which kept `global-not-found` (fires only for genuinely-unmatched URLs) from ever engaging; with it gone, unknown paths fall through to `global-not-found`. Kept `[locale]/not-found.tsx` but rewrote it bilingual via `getTranslations` (was hardcoded-Arabic — an incidental bug fix) for explicit `notFound()` from matched detail pages. **REG-28 — STATUS-CODE TABLE (prod `next start`, re-proved):**

  | route | status | `<html>` lang/dir | noindex | shell |
  |---|---|---|---|---|
  | `/xx` | **404** | ar / rtl | yes | localized full doc |
  | `/does-not-exist` | **404** | ar / rtl | yes | localized full doc |
  | `/en/does-not-exist` | **404** | en / ltr | yes | localized full doc |
  | `/xx/admin` | **404** (no gate leak) | ar / rtl | yes | localized full doc |
  | `/listing/<uuid>` (unknown) | **404** | — (default shell) | yes | localized EmptyState content, default shell |
  | `/en/listing/<uuid>` (unknown) | **404** | — (default shell) | yes | localized EmptyState content, default shell |
  | `/category/<slug>` (unknown) | **404** | — (default shell) | yes | localized EmptyState content, default shell |
  | `/en/category/<slug>` | **404** | — (default shell) | yes | localized EmptyState content, default shell |
  | `/store/<slug>` (unknown) | **404** | — (default shell) | yes | localized EmptyState content, default shell |
  | `/en/store/<slug>` | **404** | — (default shell) | yes | localized EmptyState content, default shell |

  **RESIDUAL (accepted, documented):** explicit `notFound()` from a MATCHED route (listing/category/store with an absent record) still renders localized EmptyState CONTENT inside Next's default shell — the SAME #64870 limitation, unfixable without abandoning the unprefixed-default-locale architecture. Hard-404 status + `noindex` are intact on every row (the (c) "must not regress" bar), and this is strictly BETTER than the pre-fix all-bare state (unknown routes now get the full localized document; detail-page 404s are now bilingual). **REG-27 — GUARD:** new `scripts/check-i18n-parity.mjs` (pure-Node, built-in-only, mirrors `check-no-hardcoded-arabic.mjs` style) — deep-walks both catalogs to leaf-key dot-paths, reports totals + orphans each direction, exits 1 on any orphan OR leaf-count mismatch. - **Phase 03 / T03 (2026-07-18, Opus 4.8, branch `feature/phase-03-catalog`): Search & filter `/search` — DONE, committed + PUSHED, HOLD for review verdict.** **Branch:** continued on `feature/phase-03-catalog` (on top of T02 `f6155d3`); `git pull --ff-only` was a no-op (branch never pushed → no upstream), so T02+T03 push together. **Route:** new `src/app/[locale]/(public)/search/page.tsx` (RSC) — chrome comes from the `(public)` layout, NOT touched. **QUERY** `src/features/discovery/queries/searchListings.ts` (T01 conventions: injectable `DiscoveryClient`/anon, typed returns, Zod-validated params, `.schema("betk")`, **NO service-role, NO new policies, NO new migration**). **Visibility always enforced:** `status='active' AND deleted_at IS NULL` on every branch (R-L10); the store embed is forced to `stores!inner(...)` so a listing only surfaces when its store is anon-readable — `stores_public` RLS exposes only `status='active'` stores, which is what excludes SUSPENDED-store listings (R-S07: `listings_public` alone does NOT check store status, so an active listing under a suspended store would otherwise leak). The inner join also backs the governorate/city filters (both live on `stores`). **Full-text (`search_vector`):** the `update_listing_search_vector` trigger builds the vector with the `'arabic'` config (title_ar, weight A) + `'english'` config (title_en B, description_ar C) — **NOT `unaccent`** (OD-7 §7 carry — re-verified live this session). **Arabic-diacritics probe VERIFIED LIVE + PASSING (both directions):** seeded a `مُعَلِّم` (diacritics) title + a `معلم` (plain) title; a non-diacritic query matched the diacritic title AND a diacritic query matched the plain title — the `'arabic'` TS config's normaliser folds diacritics on BOTH the stored vector and the parsed query WITHOUT unaccent. **No STOP/flag needed — the pack's "unaccent" line does NOT hold but matching works regardless; no DB-classed fix required.** A single `websearch_to_tsquery` takes ONE config, so the query config is routed by script (`pickTsConfig`: any Arabic codepoint → `'arabic'`, else `'english'`; BETK is Arabic-first, title_ar is weight-A). **R-B04 boosted ranking — RULE CONFIRMED FROM DOCS, weight NOT invented:** `BETK_PRD.md` FR-PUB-2 + `BETK_UI_SPEC.md §Search` pin the *rule* — "boosted listings rank above organic within the relevant result set / boosted-results banner at top of the relevant category" — but **no numeric weight/formula** is specified anywhere in PRD/ERD. So implemented as a **two-tier partition (no invented weight):** listings carrying an ACTIVE boost (`boosts_public`, `status='active'`) that ALSO match the query+filters form the top tier; organic matches follow. Within each tier the user-selected sort is the tiebreak. Matches the phase pack's own framing ("active-boost listings above organic within the relevant result set"). Boost-read degrades to "no boost tier" on error (enhancement, never a hard dependency); top-tier cap = 100 (R-B01 = one active boost/listing, matching subset is small). **Filters (URL-sourced, locale-neutral, preserved in URL / shareable):** `q`, `category` (uuid), `type` (product|service), `governorate` (stores.governorate slug), `city` (stores.city exact), `price_min`/`price_max`, `sort` (relevance|newest|price|popularity), `page`. Zod schema `searchListingsParamsSchema` preprocesses empty→undefined + coerces; `safeParse` at the page degrades a tampered/stale URL to defaults, never a 500. **Pagination — OFFSET for ALL sort modes (deliberate, stated), NOT T01's keyset:** the two-tier boosted/organic ordering isn't a single keyset column, and `'relevance'` has no keyset-able rank (true `ts_rank` ordering can't be expressed through the PostgREST builder without a DB rank function/generated column — a DB-classed change out of this query-only task's scope, so `'relevance'` falls back to newest-first ordering, stated not silently claimed). Offset keeps every sort consistent + correct at public-browse scale (tsvector+GIN safe to ~500K per ARCHITECTURE). Every sort carries an `id` tiebreak so offset pages don't drift on ties. **PAGE (compose-only, zero restyle):** new client `src/features/discovery/components/SearchView.tsx` composes `SearchBar` (initial query from URL), `FilterSheet` (mobile Sheet + desktop inline panel — same component, `asSheet` toggle), `FilterChips` (active filters, removable), `ListingCard` grid (via existing `ListingCardLink` — guest wishlist click → `/auth/login?returnUrl=` locale-preserving), sort `Select`, prev/next pagination, and a "featured results" label above page-1 boosted hits. URL is the single source of truth — every change writes back via `@/i18n/navigation` router; a `key` on the parsed params re-seeds the local filter draft. Titles/store names COALESCE via `localizedName`; labels via `catalogLabels` builders (`catalogFilterSheetLabels`/`catalogFilterChipsLabels`/`catalogListingBoostLabel`/`catalogSearchBarLabels`). `generateMetadata` both locales. Empty state preserves query+filters (clear-filters CTA when filters active); error state via `StripErrorCard` (retry preserves URL). New `search.*` namespace, `messages/{ar,en}.json` — **15/15 key parity**, zero orphans (overall 247/247). **Guards:** `git diff --name-only` under `src/components/ui`+`src/components/shared` = **EMPTY** (compose-only proven); zero new migrations/RLS/service-role. **VERIFY (all green):** `pnpm typecheck` clean; `pnpm lint` clean (pre-existing warnings only); all 3 guards green (`check-service-import`, `check-zod-coverage` 7 files, `check-no-hardcoded-arabic` 29 files); `pnpm test:unit` **66/66**; `pnpm build` exit 0 (`/ar/search`+`/en/search` prerendered, 4.72 kB). **Integration (staging, seeded+cleaned per T01, anon client) 4/4 PASS:** (1) keyword matches active listing + soft-deleted/draft/suspended-store excluded; (2) boosted ranks above organic even when organic is newer (R-B04); (3) Arabic diacritics both directions; (4) filter combination (type+priceMax) narrows correctly. **Runtime smoke (`next start` :3200, real staging DB):** `/ar/search?q=سماعات`, `/en/search?q=headphones`, `/search` all → 200 with `<h1>` + results heading + correct empty state (staging has 0 matching listings → empty path exercised for real). **Files:** new `search/page.tsx`, `SearchView.tsx`, `searchListings.ts`, `tests/integration/discovery.search.test.ts`; edited `src/validations/discovery.ts` (+`searchListingsParamsSchema`/`searchSortSchema`/`searchListingTypeSchema`/`pickTsConfig`), `src/features/discovery/types/index.ts` (+`SearchListingItem`/`SearchResultPage`), `src/features/discovery/index.ts` (barrel exports), `messages/{ar,en}.json` (+`search.*`), `docs/.../PHASE_03_CATALOG.md` (synced the stored T03 prompt to the executed one — record only, no scope change). **Committed + PUSHED. HOLD for the review-security + review-ui gate verdicts. Next: on PASS → PR/merge → Phase 03 T04.**

- **R5 (2026-07-16, Opus 4.8): promoted `rls-smoke` to a conditionally-required gate on migration-touching PRs — REG-08 CLOSED. CI-only change; ZERO test-code change.** **Why:** RLS is the sole authz boundary and the policy set stands at 39 (post-T01-FIX), but the green smoke harness never ran on the PRs that change policies. **What changed (only `.github/workflows/ci.yml` + docs):** the `rls-smoke` job's trigger `if:` now also fires on `pull_request` (in addition to `workflow_dispatch` + develop-push). Because GitHub has no job-level `paths:` filter, the job runs on EVERY PR and self-gates internally via a new **`Detect migration changes`** step: on `workflow_dispatch`/develop-push it always runs (explicit opt-in); on a PR it `git diff --name-only <base> <head> -- supabase/migrations/` and sets `run=true` only if a migration file changed, else logs an explicit **"no migration changes … skipping RLS smoke"** line and sets `run=false` (a **logged no-op, not a silent job-level skip** — so the check always posts a status and branch protection can mark it *required*, making it BLOCKING exactly on migration-touching PRs). Checkout uses `fetch-depth: 0` so the base SHA diff resolves. All downstream steps (pnpm/node/cache setup, verify-secrets, run-suite) are gated `if: steps.detect.outputs.run == 'true'`. **Fail-loudly retained + strengthened:** the missing-staging-secrets check still `exit 1`s (now with an extra "migration-touching PR must be smoke-tested" error line), so a migration PR from a fork — which lacks secrets — fails VISIBLY, never a silent pass. **Residual-risk documented in the job comment:** the harness mints/tears down real staging GoTrue auth users → concurrent runs could collide; mitigated by (a) the harness's per-run unique-suffix on unique columns + prefix-sweep of crashed-run leftovers (`tests/integration/rls.smoke.test.ts`) and (b) the existing `concurrency: ci-${{ github.ref }}` cancel-in-progress (a PR never races itself; cross-PR races are covered by (a)). Job renamed `RLS smoke (opt-in, staging)` → `RLS smoke (staging)`. **Not run this task** — the next migration-touching PR proves it (per the task's explicit instruction). **CI:** YAML validated (parses clean); no `src/`/test change → typecheck/guards/build unaffected. **Docs updated:** `docs/08-deployment/CICD_PIPELINE.md` (new conditionally-required RLS-smoke rule + migrations cross-reference), this file (REG-08 closed + this entry), journal. **Files:** `.github/workflows/ci.yml`, `docs/08-deployment/CICD_PIPELINE.md`, this file, journal. **Next: unchanged — the DS token-apply/regeneration + the standing HOLDs before Phase 03 / T02; branch protection owner to mark `RLS smoke (staging)` as a required check.**

DB/ops-classed; ONE new migration; ledger stays 1:1.** Prereq checked FIRST: R1 ledger alignment confirmed (17/17 before this task). **Investigated (read-only) before changing anything:** `show cron.timezone;` → `'GMT'`; `pg_settings` → `context='postmaster'`, `source='default'` for `cron.timezone` (server-TZ `TimeZone`/`log_timezone`=`UTC`). A postmaster-context GUC can only change at server start (config file + restart) — NOT settable persistently via SQL on hosted Supabase — so the single-setting `cron.timezone='Africa/Cairo'` fix was unavailable → rescheduled (the expected path). **Migration `20260716130533_reschedule_daily_cron_utc.sql`** `cron.unschedule` + `cron.schedule`'d ONLY the 3 DAILY jobs (command bodies re-supplied VERBATIM from `20260622083154_cron.sql`; job names unchanged). **DST reasoning:** Egypt = UTC+2 winter (standard) / UTC+3 summer (~Apr–Oct); a fixed UTC expression drifts ±1h seasonally. Anchored each on standard time (UTC+2) → exact Cairo intent in winter, +1h in summer, all verified to stay in the overnight Cairo window (post-midnight ~00:00–04:00) in BOTH seasons: **recalculate-seller-levels** `0 0 * * *` = 02:00 winter / 03:00 summer (intent ≈02:00); **daily-platform-snapshot** `5 22 * * *` = 00:05 winter / 01:05 summer (intent ≈00:05 — 22:05 UTC is within the same UTC calendar day, so the body's `CURRENT_DATE-1` accounting is unchanged from the original 00:05-UTC firing); **lift-temp-suspensions** `0 1 * * *` = 03:00 winter / 04:00 summer (intent ≈03:00). **The 3 interval jobs (`expire-boosts` `*/15 * * * *`, `dispute-sla-alert` `0 * * * *`, `cleanup-otp-tokens` `30 * * * *`) are timezone-independent → LEFT UNTOUCHED.** **Source parity:** `BETK_DATABASE_SCHEMA.sql` cron section header + the 3 daily `cron.schedule` expressions/comments updated to the new UTC schedules with dual-season Cairo times; the original applied `20260622083154_cron.sql` was **NOT** edited. **Applied** via MCP `apply_migration` (CLI unusable here — `supabase.exe` fails `config.toml` validation on the missing `SEND_SMS_HOOK_SECRET` hook secret; same authorized MCP-fallback path R2 used). - **R2 (2026-07-16, Opus 4.8): backfilled the `decrement_stock_on_confirm` trigger — open-issue #4 CLOSED. DB/security-classed; source + 2 migrations; ledger stays 1:1.** Spec read FIRST (ERD §7 + R-L05/R-L06/R-N06): all core semantics were specified → no STOP-flag. **Behavior:** `AFTER UPDATE OF status ON betk.orders FOR EACH ROW WHEN (OLD.status IS DISTINCT FROM NEW.status AND NEW.status='confirmed')` → for each `order_items` row of that order, decrement the listing's `stock_qty` by the ordered quantity (aggregated per listing), and flip an `active` listing to `sold_out` when it reaches exactly 0 (R-L06). **Edge decisions (grounded, not improvised):** untracked stock (`stock_qty IS NULL` — services / made-to-order) is skipped (`WHERE stock_qty IS NOT NULL`); oversell is caught by the existing `CHECK (stock_qty >= 0)` which rolls the confirm back (no clamp); only `active`→`sold_out` per the R-L06/R-L07 state machine (draft/paused/removed untouched); fires only on the transition INTO `confirmed` (no double-decrement on later transitions); R-N06 (sold_out→restock CTA) is UI, out of trigger scope. **Security:** `SECURITY DEFINER` + `SET search_path = betk, public` pinned (fixes advisor 0011 pattern for the new fn); the default `PUBLIC` EXECUTE grant is REVOKED (migration `20260716125122`) so it is NOT RPC-exposed → advisor 0028/0029 do NOT flag it (verified: only pre-existing `is_admin`/`my_store_id` remain). Trigger still fires (EXECUTE grant is irrelevant to trigger invocation). **Applied** via MCP `apply_migration` (CLI unlinked — no creds; same authorized path as T01-FIX); recorded versions `20260716124323`/`20260716125122`; local files named to match → ledger↔local re-verified **17/17 1:1**, no R1-style drift. **Live-verified** `pg_trigger` (trigger on `betk.orders`) + `pg_proc` (`prosecdef=t`, `proconfig={search_path=betk, public}`, `proacl={postgres=X/postgres}`). **Integration** `tests/integration/orders.stockDecrement.test.ts` **6/6 PASS** (exact→0+sold_out, partial→stays active, multi-item per-qty, NULL-skip, fires-once, oversell-rollback), zero staging residue (post-run counts all 0). **CI:** `pnpm typecheck` exit 0; `pnpm lint` exit 0 (pre-existing warnings only); 3 guards green. **Files:** `docs/03-database/BETK_DATABASE_SCHEMA.sql` (5th trigger + REVOKE), `supabase/migrations/20260716124323_*.sql` + `20260716125122_*.sql` (new), `tests/integration/orders.stockDecrement.test.ts` (new), this file + journal. **Next: unchanged — HOLD on the T01-FIX security-review verdict + T00-LAND catalog-kit gate before Phase 03 / T02.**

- **Phase 02 / T08-FIX (2026-06-30, Opus 4.8): OTP ≤5-attempt limiter re-anchored to the OTP lifecycle — open-issue #12 RESOLVED.** Fixes the T08 hard failure where wall-clock 60s epoch bucketing let a mid-bucket OTP straddle a boundary into a second `otp_tokens` row (~10 attempts on one valid code). **Removed** `challengeWindowStart` + the `betk-otp-challenge:{phone}:{windowStartMs}` derived hash. **Added** `createOtpChallenge(phone)` — called at SEND time in `sendOtp` + `sendPhoneOtp` AFTER a successful GoTrue send (so a rate-limited resend never supersedes a still-valid challenge); it supersedes prior active rows (`is_used=true`; deterministic freshest-wins) then inserts ONE row (`expires_at=now+60s`, `attempt_count=0`, opaque random `token_hash`=`randomBytes(32)` hex — never the OTP, not used for lookup). **Reworked** `recordOtpAttempt(phone)` (still called BEFORE GoTrue verify, so wrong/expired codes count): selects the MOST-RECENT active row (`expires_at>now AND is_used=false`, `order expires_at desc limit 1`), increments THAT row; no active row → reject (allowed:false, attemptsUsed:0); `attempt_count>=5` → reject. `markOtpUsed` closes the active row on success. Same row spans the full 60s, so an epoch-boundary crossing can no longer reset the counter; `chk_otp_attempts CHECK(<=5)` stays the DB backstop. **AC-AUTH-2 no-leak preserved** (limiter never receives/logs/persists the raw OTP; only the phone + counts). On a `createOtpChallenge` failure the send action fails closed (generic Arabic error) since verify would reject anyway. **Both verify paths covered** (sign-in `verifyOtp` + phone-capture `verifyPhoneOtp` call the same primitives — reuse, not fork). **Regression proof — `tests/unit/otpLimiter.straddle.test.ts` (new, mocked service client + faked clock):** OTP issued boundary−30s, attempts driven on BOTH sides of the 60s epoch boundary against the SAME still-valid OTP — the test FAILS on the old wall-clock code (6th wrongly allowed=true, captured before the fix) and PASSES on the fix (6th rejected); plus lifecycle/supersede/expiry/markOtpUsed assertions (8 tests). **CI: pnpm typecheck clean (exit 0); pnpm lint clean (only pre-existing T11 service console warnings); check-service-import clean; check-zod-coverage clean (7 files); pnpm test:unit 40/40 (+8); `tests/integration/auth.otp.test.ts` 9/9 PASS against staging (1 manual happy-path skipped) — proves createOtpChallenge→recordOtpAttempt→markOtpUsed + a resend leaving exactly ONE active row + no-challenge rejection on the real `otp_tokens` table.** **Files:** `src/services/otpLimiter.ts` (rework), `src/features/auth/actions/sendOtp.ts` + `src/features/auth/actions/sendPhoneOtp.ts` (+`createOtpChallenge` after send), `tests/unit/otpLimiter.straddle.test.ts` (new), `tests/integration/auth.otp.test.ts` (lifecycle contract + supersede/no-challenge/markOtpUsed cases), `tests/unit/auth.unit.test.ts` (§4 token_hash → opaque-nonce assertions). Next: T08 RE-RUN (exit verification + Phase 02 sign-off). Do NOT start Phase 03.

- **Phase 02 / T08 (2026-06-30, Opus 4.8): PHASE 02 EXIT VERIFICATION — SIGN-OFF BLOCKED (1 HARD FAILURE).** Verified the PHASE_02 DoD + AC-AUTH-2 against the committed tree + live staging (`sojmjvohiziapiwkzsjg`). **PASS/FAIL per line below; all but one PASS.** **HARD FAILURE (blocks sign-off) — open-issue #12, the ≤5-attempt limiter window straddle:** `src/services/otpLimiter.ts` buckets attempts by ABSOLUTE wall-clock 60s windows (`challengeWindowStart = Math.floor(now/60000)*60000`; `token_hash = sha256("betk-otp-challenge:{phone}:{windowStartMs}")`), computed from `Date.now()` at *verify* time — NOT anchored to the OTP's issuance/lifecycle. A 60s-lived GoTrue OTP is almost always issued mid-bucket, so its valid lifetime straddles a 60s epoch boundary: attempts before the boundary land in row A (counts 1–5), attempts after it land in a *fresh* row B (counts 1–5) while the SAME OTP is still valid → up to ~10 verify attempts on one code. `chk_otp_attempts CHECK(attempt_count<=5)` caps each row at 5 but does NOT stop the second row, so it is not a backstop for the straddle. GoTrue has no per-token verify counter (only per-IP throttle, ADR-010 §4), so the app-layer counter is the sole owner of AC-AUTH-2 clause 4 and the guarantee is broken. **Fix (recommended, queued as T08-FIX): re-anchor the counter to the actual OTP lifecycle — create one `otp_tokens` row at send time (`sendOtp`/`sendPhoneOtp`) with `expires_at = issuance+60s`, and at verify select the active (non-expired, not-used) row for the phone and increment IT regardless of wall-clock bucket.** Affects BOTH the T02 sign-in path and the T07 phone-capture path (shared limiter). This was the explicit T02 carry-forward; T08 confirms it as a real defeat, not theoretical. **PARKED items confirmed STILL parked against live DB (`pg_policies`):** `betk.users` has ONLY `users_self` PERMISSIVE SELECT (no self-UPDATE/INSERT policy — all writes still service-role per T06 decision); `orders` has only `orders_access` SELECT + `orders_phone_gate` RESTRICTIVE INSERT (NO permissive ownership INSERT — default-denied, Phase 07 owns); `seller_profiles` has only `sp_select`/`sp_update` + `seller_profiles_phone_gate` RESTRICTIVE INSERT (NO permissive INSERT — Phase 04 owns); `payouts` retains the working pair (`payouts_insert` permissive + `payouts_phone_gate` restrictive). The 3 OD-4 phone-gate WITH CHECKs (`EXISTS(users WHERE id=auth.uid() AND phone_number IS NOT NULL)`) are byte-identical and intact from Phase 01. `otp_tokens` has NO client-readable policy (service-only) and NO plaintext-OTP column (columns: id, phone_number, token_hash, expires_at, is_used, attempt_count, created_at) → raw OTP cannot be persisted there; `recordOtpAttempt` never even receives the OTP (only the phone). **Entry debts confirmed CLEARED:** GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID/SECRET are `z.string().min(1)` (required, fail-fast) in `configs/env.ts`; `sanitizeReturnUrl` rejects `//`, `http(s)://`, non-`/`-prefix, backslash, AND ASCII control chars (0x00–0x1F) and is wired at both middleware redirect sites + verify/callback; `Sentry.setUser({id})` set in `SentryProvider` (client) + every auth Server Action (server scope, id-only no PII); all server-side PostHog capture imports `@/services/posthog.server`. **All 7 Server Actions Zod-validated** (sendOtp/verifyOtp/sendPhoneOtp/verifyPhoneOtp/completeProfile/updateProfile/deactivateAccount) — `check-zod-coverage` green (7 files). **CARRY-FORWARD (named, owned) — live OAuth consent round-trip:** the T03 headless tests cover find-or-create ONLY (admin-minted users); a real PKCE consent → cookie round-trip CANNOT run headlessly in this env (interactive Google consent screen, no browser-automation creds) → carried as a NAMED pre-launch E2E item (open-issue #13), NOT re-asserted via the find-or-create half. **PRE-LAUNCH GATE recorded (not a code failure):** handset SMS delivery still unconfirmed — server chain green (`sms_delivered`, sender `3MS EGY`) but device delivery + Arabic/Unicode route + live credit pending TorvoSMS → THE blocking pre-launch item for phone-OTP. **CI: pnpm typecheck clean (exit 0); pnpm lint clean (only pre-existing T11 service console warnings); check-service-import clean; check-zod-coverage clean (7 files); pnpm test:unit 32/32.** No code/schema changes in T08 (verification only). **Next: T08-FIX (limiter re-anchor) → re-verify → sign off Phase 02 → Phase 03.**

- **Phase 02 / T07 (2026-06-30, Opus 4.8): Verified-phone transaction gate (app layer) + phone-capture flow — DONE.** Built the canonical app-layer half of the OD-4 phone gate (the RLS WITH CHECK half is live since Phase 01) plus the UX that lets a phone-NULL Google user obtain a phone. **(1) Shared gate `requireVerifiedPhone()`** (`src/features/auth/queries/requireVerifiedPhone.ts`, server-only) — the single transaction-time gate checkout [Phase 07], become-seller [Phase 04], payout [Phase 13] will consume (do NOT re-implement). Loads the user FRESH (`getUserRowById`, service-role read — authoritative current row) and rejects in order: R-A05 `deleted_at` → `UserDeactivatedError`, R-A05 `status != 'active'` → `UserNotActiveError`, then `phone_number IS NULL` → `PhoneRequiredError` (so a deactivated user with a phone is still blocked as deactivated, never funnelled to "add phone"). Throws typed `AuthUserError` subclasses (caller routes). Split into `requireVerifiedPhoneForUser(userId)` (testable core, session-less) + `requireVerifiedPhone()` (wrapper deriving the id from live `getUser()`); `NotAuthenticatedError` for no-session. Exported from the auth barrel for downstream phases. **(2) Phone-capture flow** — authenticated phone-NULL user adds + verifies a phone. REUSES the T02 OTP primitives (no second OTP path): `sendPhoneOtp` (`supabase.auth.updateUser({ phone })` = GoTrue phone-change OTP; eligibility-gated to active + phone-NULL; UX pre-check `isPhoneNumberTaken`) and `verifyPhoneOtp` (REUSES `recordOtpAttempt`/`markOtpUsed` ≤5 limiter incremented BEFORE GoTrue; `verifyOtp({ type:'phone_change' })`). **Ordering is structural: the betk.users phone write happens ONLY AFTER `verifyOtp` succeeds — never on send, never optimistically.** **(3) WRITE PATH = service-role** (`setUserPhoneNumber(id, phone)` in `src/services/authUsers.ts`, per the T06 settled precedent — betk.users has no self-UPDATE policy and one is NOT added) — sets **ONLY** `phone_number`; `auth_provider` stays `'google'` (origin, not capability); `role`/`status`/`deleted_at` never touched; keyed to the session-verified uid. **(4) UNIQUE collision handled at WRITE time via the 23505 catch** (`uq_users_phone`) → returns `{ conflict: "phone_taken" }` → clean Arabic "رقم الهاتف مستخدم بالفعل في حساب آخر"; **never merges accounts**. The pre-check SELECT (`isPhoneNumberTaken`) is UX-only (TOCTOU race) — the 23505 catch is the authoritative guard, per the task. **(5) First live consumer + proof:** `/auth/phone` RSC page (`src/app/(auth)/auth/phone/page.tsx`, the T05 "add phone" link target) calls `requireVerifiedPhone()` and routes every outcome — passes (already has phone) → `/account`; `PhoneRequiredError` → render `PhoneCaptureForm`; `NotAuthenticatedError` → `/auth/login`; R-A05 → `/blocked`. `PhoneCaptureForm` ("use client", two-step send→verify via two `useActionState`). `/account` page entry-point cast updated (route now live). **PARKED policies confirmed NOT added** — orders/seller_profiles permissive ownership INSERT remain Phase 07 / Phase 04 (open-issue 1); T07 is app-layer gate + phone-capture ONLY. Zod on both actions (`phoneInputSchema` send, `otpVerifySchema` verify); Sentry tagged `'auth-phone-gate'`; PostHog `phone_captured`. **Files:** `src/services/authUsers.ts` (+`setUserPhoneNumber` w/ 23505 catch + `isPhoneNumberTaken` pre-check), `src/features/auth/queries/requireVerifiedPhone.ts` (new), `src/features/auth/actions/sendPhoneOtp.ts` (new), `src/features/auth/actions/verifyPhoneOtp.ts` (new), `src/features/auth/index.ts` (+exports), `src/app/(auth)/auth/phone/page.tsx` (new) + `_components/PhoneCaptureForm.tsx` (new), `src/app/(buyer)/account/page.tsx` (entry-point comment/cast), `tests/integration/auth.phoneGate.test.ts` (new). **Tests — 4/4 PASS against staging** (STAGING_GUARD-gated): T07-a phone-NULL Google user blocked → `PhoneRequiredError`; T07-b after `setUserPhoneNumber` (what verify writes) the SAME user passes the gate, `auth_provider` still `'google'`; T07-c duplicate-phone rejected via the **23505 path** (`{ conflict: "phone_taken" }`, not pre-check) — loser stays NULL, owner keeps the number, no merge; T07-d a DEACTIVATED user **with** a phone is still blocked → `UserDeactivatedError` (proves the R-A05 half). **CI: pnpm typecheck clean (exit 0); pnpm lint clean (no new errors — pre-existing T11 service console warnings only); check-service-import clean; check-zod-coverage clean (7 files); pnpm test:unit 32/32; auth.phoneGate.test 4/4 PASS against staging.** Next: T08 Phase 02 exit verification (Opus).

- **Phase 02 / T06 (2026-06-30, Opus 4.8): Account deactivation (OD-2, DEACTIVATE-only) — DONE.** **DECISION = Option A (service-role helper), grounded against live staging.** Live check (`pg_policy` + `role_table_grants`): `betk.users` has exactly ONE policy — `users_self` **SELECT-only** (`USING (id=auth.uid()) OR betk.is_admin()`, no WITH CHECK); no UPDATE policy → all authenticated UPDATEs default-denied. BUT the table-level grant to `authenticated` is **`UPDATE` on ALL columns**. **Option B (scoped permissive UPDATE policy) REJECTED:** a plain `USING (id=auth.uid())` UPDATE policy would combine with that all-column grant to let a user rewrite `role`/`status`/`phone_number` on their own row (privilege escalation — self-promote to admin, self-clear suspension, phone hijack). Safe B would require `REVOKE UPDATE ON betk.users FROM authenticated` + `GRANT UPDATE(deleted_at,anonymized_at)` (a whole-table grant change that also prematurely opens `anonymized_at`, reserved post-MVP) or a WITH CHECK/trigger rejecting other-column changes — more surface, more to get wrong. **Option A keeps the write column-scoped in code with zero schema/grant/policy change; RLS stays the authz boundary; consistent with `updateLastLoginAt`.** No concrete requirement found that the user must self-deactivate under their own session (action runs server-side with the verified uid; the R-A05 gate is independent of how `deleted_at` was written). Recorded as a FINDING, not a policy to apply — nothing left pending for review. **Files:** `src/validations/account.ts` (+`deactivateAccountSchema` — `confirm: z.literal("DEACTIVATE")`, explicit confirmation enforced server-side); `src/services/authUsers.ts` (+`deactivateAccount(id)` — service-role UPDATE setting **ONLY** `deleted_at=now()` keyed to `.eq("id", verifiedUid)`; never `anonymized_at`/`role`/`status`/`phone_number`; never another id; idempotent); `src/features/buyer-account/actions/deactivateAccount.ts` (new "use server" — Zod-validates confirm; reads uid from live GoTrue session (never form); calls `deactivateAccount(user.id)`; `Sentry.setUser`+`captureTaggedError`; PostHog `account_deactivated`; **`await supabase.auth.signOut()` completes BEFORE `redirect("/")`** — user holds a valid session cookie until signOut; signOut error captured but redirect proceeds since `deleted_at` already gates them); `src/app/(buyer)/account/_components/DeactivateAccountForm.tsx` (new "use client" — two-step reveal → confirmation checkbox carrying the `DEACTIVATE` token + confirm/cancel; `useActionState`; Arabic; error-only render since success redirects); `src/app/(buyer)/account/page.tsx` (+deactivate section); `src/features/buyer-account/index.ts` (+`deactivateAccount`/`DeactivateAccountResult` exports). **R-A05 LOOP PROVEN AT THE REAL GATE** (`tests/integration/account.deactivate.test.ts`, 3 tests, STAGING_GUARD-gated, all PASS against staging): T06-1 `deactivateAccount` sets ONLY `deleted_at` — `role`/`status`(still 'active')/`phone_number` unchanged, `anonymized_at` stays NULL (no anonymization in MVP); T06-2 after deactivation `findOrCreateUser` (the SAME primitive verifyOtp + `/auth/callback` call) REJECTS with `UserDeactivatedError` and does NOT clear `deleted_at` — a deactivated user cannot transact back in; this is the real verify/callback gate, not merely a middleware redirect (middleware `deleted_at IS NOT NULL → /blocked` is the defence-in-depth layer); T06-3 deactivating user A leaves user B's `deleted_at` NULL (action only ever sets `auth.uid()`). **CI: pnpm typecheck clean (exit 0); pnpm lint clean (no new errors — pre-existing T11 service console warnings only); check-service-import clean; check-zod-coverage clean (5 files); pnpm test:unit 32/32; account.deactivate.test 3/3 PASS against staging.**

- **Phase 01 / T10 (2026-06-23, Sonnet 4.6):** Auth middleware skeleton — DONE. `src/middleware.ts` (@supabase/ssr cookie-bound `createServerClient`, ARCHITECTURE §4) + `app/blocked/page.tsx`. **Sentry untouched.** `getUser()` (revalidates, not `getSession()`) before gating; mutable `NextResponse.next({request})` carries refreshed cookies (copied onto every redirect). Gate by URL prefix (route groups are URL-invisible): `/admin*`=admin, `/seller*`=seller, `/account /wishlist /orders /inbox /notifications /checkout /disputes`=buyer, all else (`/` `/search` `/category` `/listing` `/store` `/auth/*` `/blocked`)=public. Public → refresh + pass, **no DB read**. Protected unauth → `/auth/login?returnUrl=…`. Authed → **one indexed PK lookup** `betk.users(role,status,deleted_at)` (RLS `users_self`). **R-A05** block (`status!='active' OR deleted_at NOT NULL`) → `/blocked`. Admin gate = role∈(admin,superadmin); seller gate = role='seller' + **R-S04** non-active `seller_profiles.status` → `/seller/status` (skipped on that route to avoid loop). - **Phase 01 / T14 (2026-06-23, Opus 4.8): PHASE 01 EXIT VERIFICATION — PASS, signed off.** Verified against live staging (`sojmjvohiziapiwkzsjg`) + committed tree. **PASS lines:** RTL boot (`<html dir="rtl" lang="ar">` + Cairo/IBM Plex Sans Arabic/IBM Plex Mono CSS vars + full HSL token set in globals.css); **43 tables** (betk 41 + betk_analytics 2); users freeze deltas (`phone_number` varchar nullable + UNIQUE `uq_users_phone`; `auth_provider` NOT NULL DEFAULT `'phone'`; `deleted_at`/`anonymized_at` nullable tstz); **4 triggers** live (`trg_dispute_sla`, `trg_listing_search_vector`, `trg_recalculate_rating`, `trg_review_edit_deadline`) — `decrement_stock_on_confirm` still ABSENT (known T05 source gap, NOT invented, still owed to source); 3 OD-4 **RESTRICTIVE** phone-gates confirmed on `orders`/`seller_profiles`/`payouts` INSERT (identical `WITH CHECK EXISTS(users WHERE id=auth.uid() AND phone_number IS NOT NULL)`); 2 helpers (`is_admin`, `my_store_id`); **6 pg_cron** jobs all active (expire-boosts, recalculate-seller-levels, daily-platform-snapshot, dispute-sla-alert, lift-temp-suspensions, cleanup-otp-tokens); seeds (3 boost_packages, 11 admin_settings, 39 categories); `types.ts` current (real generated set, betk_analytics + 34 enums present; CI types-drift gate green per T13); middleware gates by group+role + `/blocked`; 6 services scaffolded (resend/posthog/sentry/whatsapp/sms/courier + posthog.server); CI gates present (unit + service-import + zod-coverage + types-drift; rls-smoke opt-in). **DOC CORRECTIONS (not blockers):** (1) **RLS policy count = 34 TOTAL but the attribution in prior summaries was wrong** — actual is `betk` 32 (29 PERMISSIVE + 3 RESTRICTIVE) + `betk_analytics` 2 = 34, NOT "31+3 all in betk". (2) **Index count = 41 non-constraint indexes** (40 `idx_*` + 1 partial-unique `uq_active_boost_per_listing`), not the 34 estimated in the DoD/ERD §4 — more indexes than estimated, no missing-index failure. (3) **pg_cron timezone:** migration `…_cron.sql` documents all 6 schedules as Cairo-local in comments but stores bare cron expressions (e.g. `0 2 * * *`); pg_cron evaluates in the cluster TZ (UTC on Supabase), so daily jobs will fire ~3h off Cairo unless `CRON_TZ=`/`cron.timezone` is set — ops/deploy hardening, not a Phase-01 blocker. **No hard failures (no missing table, no broken freeze delta, default-deny holds per T08) → Phase 01 SIGNED OFF.**

- **Phase 02 / T02-FIX (2026-06-29, Sonnet 4.6): T02 close-out hardening — SIGNED OFF.** (1) Backslash vector: `src/validations/returnUrl.ts` now rejects any candidate containing `\` (browsers treat `/\evil.com` as `//evil.com`) and ASCII control chars (0x00–0x1F — covers `\r`, `\n`, `\t` header-injection). (2) Test placement: moved all pure-unit assertions (returnUrl guard, phoneInputSchema, otpVerifySchema, token_hash SHA-256 format) from `tests/integration/auth.otp.test.ts` into **`tests/unit/auth.unit.test.ts`** (21 tests); integration file now contains only DB-touching tests (otp_tokens limiter 3×, updateLastLoginAt 1×, R-A05 2×, manual happy-path 1×). `pnpm test:unit` now reports **32/32 passed** (was 11/11 — 21 new pure-unit auth assertions). `pnpm typecheck` clean; `pnpm lint` clean (no new errors).

`src/middleware.ts` (wired `sanitizeReturnUrl` at both returnUrl redirect sites — ENTRY DEBT 1 cleared); `src/services/authUsers.ts` (extended: `updateLastLoginAt` + `resolvePostAuthRedirect` — both service-role, no betk.users self-UPDATE policy exists); `src/services/otpLimiter.ts` (new: `recordOtpAttempt` + `markOtpUsed` backed by `betk.otp_tokens` — AC-AUTH-2 clause 4 ≤5 attempts/token; SHA-256 nonce keyed to phone+challenge-window; NEVER stores the OTP); `src/features/auth/actions/sendOtp.ts` (new: Server Action, Zod validated, signInWithOtp, rate-limit error Arabic); `src/features/auth/actions/verifyOtp.ts` (new: Server Action, Zod validated, recordOtpAttempt BEFORE GoTrue, verifyOtp, markOtpUsed, findOrCreateUser, updateLastLoginAt, R-A05 deactivated-block + session-clear, Sentry.setUser server-scope, PostHog captureServerEvent, resolvePostAuthRedirect role-route, redirect as Route); `src/app/(auth)/auth/verify/page.tsx` + `_components/OtpVerifyForm.tsx` (new: 6-digit OTP, masked phone display, hidden phone+returnUrl fields); `src/app/_providers/SentryProvider.tsx` (extended: Sentry.setUser({ id }) via getUser + onAuthStateChange subscription, no PII — ENTRY DEBT 2 cleared); `src/features/auth/index.ts` (re-exported sendOtp + verifyOtp); `tests/integration/auth.otp.test.ts` (new: returnUrl guard, phoneInputSchema, otpVerifySchema, otp_tokens limiter 5-then-block, updateLastLoginAt, R-A05 deactivated/suspended, manual happy-path gated by BETK_OTP_HAPPY_PATH=1). **CI: pnpm typecheck clean, pnpm lint clean (no new errors), check-service-import clean, check-zod-coverage clean, pnpm test:unit 11/11 pass.** FINDING confirmed (not resolved): `betk.users` still has no permissive self-UPDATE policy → `last_login_at`, `deleted_at`, `phone_number` all use service-role path. Surface at T06 (deactivate) gate for policy review.

- **Phase 02 / T01 (2026-06-24, Opus 4.8): Auth model DECIDED + SIGNED OFF.** ADR-010 = Model A (GoTrue-canonical): Supabase Auth owns phone-OTP issuance/verify + Google OAuth + sessions; `betk.users` is the find-or-create mirror keyed 1:1 to `auth.users.id`. AC-AUTH-2 clause map: (1) "never-persist-raw-OTP" ✅ structural — app never writes the OTP; GoTrue stores a hash; verify action (T02) handles the code transiently, no log/Sentry/DB write. (2) 60s expiry + one-active/60s ✅ GoTrue `SMS_OTP_EXP` default 60 + `SMS_MAX_FREQUENCY=60s`. (3) expired/used rejected ✅ GoTrue single-use invalidation. (4) "≤5 attempts/token" ⚠️ NOT covered by GoTrue (verified in `internal/api/verify.go` — only a per-IP throttle exists, not a per-token counter) → app-layer limiter in T02 verify Server Action, backed by `betk.otp_tokens` (`attempt_count` + `CHECK(attempt_count <= 5)`), storing only an opaque SHA-256 server nonce in `token_hash` + phone + expiry — never the OTP. Table fate: `betk.otp_tokens` = app-layer attempt-limiter/audit (hourly `cleanup-otp-tokens` cron purges it); `betk.sessions` = UNUSED in MVP (GoTrue owns sessions; OD-5 froze UI; kept for parity). `findOrCreateUser` primitive live: `src/features/auth/queries/findOrCreateUser.ts` (Zod-validated, finds-by-id-or-creates, re-checks R-A05 `status='active' AND deleted_at IS NULL`, service-role path for INSERT via `src/services/authUsers.ts`). `GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID`/`GOOGLE_CLIENT_SECRET` re-tightened to required (entry debt CLEARED). Files: `docs/02-architecture/ADR.md` (+ADR-010), `supabase/config.toml`, `src/validations/auth.ts`, `src/services/authUsers.ts`, `src/features/auth/queries/findOrCreateUser.ts`, `src/features/auth/index.ts`, `src/configs/env.ts`. T02 (Sonnet) is unblocked.

    - **`betk.users` no-self-UPDATE-policy FINDING (T01/T02 carry-forward) — RESOLVED-BY-DECISION for the deactivate path (Option A).** The gap remains the standing pattern: `last_login_at` (T02), `deleted_at` (T06), and now `phone_number` (T07 `setUserPhoneNumber`) all use the service-role path. The reason a scoped permissive UPDATE policy is NOT added: the table-level `GRANT UPDATE` to `authenticated` covers ALL columns, so any `USING (id=auth.uid())` UPDATE policy is a privilege-escalation vector (role/status/phone self-rewrite) until that grant is revoked + re-scoped to `UPDATE(deleted_at,anonymized_at)`. Keep using the service-role path for `betk.users` writes unless/until a future task deliberately re-scopes the grant.
11. **~~T07: verified-phone transaction gate + phone-capture~~ — ✅ DONE 2026-06-30 (T07, Opus 4.8).** `requireVerifiedPhone()` (`src/features/auth`) is the canonical app-layer gate for Phase 04/07/13 (FRESH load + R-A05-then-phone-NULL; throws typed errors). Phone-capture flow (`/auth/phone` + `sendPhoneOtp`/`verifyPhoneOtp`) reuses the T02 OTP primitives + ≤5 limiter — no second OTP path; betk.users `phone_number` written ONLY after `verifyOtp` succeeds, via service-role `setUserPhoneNumber` (auth_provider stays 'google'); UNIQUE collision caught at write via 23505 → clean Arabic, never merges. `/auth/phone` page is the first live consumer + proof. Parked orders/seller_profiles INSERT policies confirmed NOT added (open-issue 1).
12. **~~🔴 HARD FAILURE — OTP ≤5-attempt limiter window STRADDLE~~ — ✅ RESOLVED 2026-06-30 (T08-FIX, Opus 4.8).** Root cause (T08): `src/services/otpLimiter.ts` keyed `attempt_count` on an absolute wall-clock 60s epoch bucket (`challengeWindowStart`), unrelated to OTP issuance; a mid-bucket OTP's 60s life straddled the next boundary → a second `otp_tokens` row → 5 more attempts (~10 on one valid code), defeating AC-AUTH-2 clause 4. **Fix: limiter re-anchored to the OTP lifecycle.** `challengeWindowStart`/wall-clock bucketing REMOVED. New `createOtpChallenge(phone)` is called at SEND time (`sendOtp` + `sendPhoneOtp`, after a successful GoTrue send) — it supersedes any still-active row for the phone (`is_used=true`, deterministic "freshest wins") then inserts ONE row with `expires_at = now+60s`, `attempt_count=0`, opaque random `token_hash` (`randomBytes(32)` hex — never the OTP, not used for lookup). `recordOtpAttempt(phone)` (called BEFORE GoTrue verify) now selects the MOST-RECENT active row (`expires_at > now AND is_used=false`) and increments THAT row; no active row → reject; ≥5 → reject. `markOtpUsed` closes the active row on success. Same row serves the whole 60s lifetime, so crossing an epoch boundary cannot reset the counter. `chk_otp_attempts CHECK(<=5)` remains the hard backstop. **Both `verifyOtp` (sign-in) and `verifyPhoneOtp` (phone-capture) covered — shared limiter.** **Regression proof:** `tests/unit/otpLimiter.straddle.test.ts` fakes the clock (OTP issued boundary−30s, attempts on both sides of the 60s epoch boundary) — FAILS on the old wall-clock code (6th wrongly allowed), PASSES on the fix (6th rejected) + lifecycle/supersede/expiry/markOtpUsed coverage. CI: typecheck clean; lint clean; check-zod-coverage 7; **test:unit 40/40** (+8); **auth.otp integration 9/9 PASS against staging** (createOtpChallenge→recordOtpAttempt→markOtpUsed + supersede single-active-row proven). Files: `src/services/otpLimiter.ts` (rework), `src/features/auth/actions/sendOtp.ts` + `sendPhoneOtp.ts` (+`createOtpChallenge` after send), `tests/unit/otpLimiter.straddle.test.ts` (new), `tests/integration/auth.otp.test.ts` (lifecycle contract), `tests/unit/auth.unit.test.ts` (token_hash → opaque nonce).
13b. **~~🔴 HARD-BLOCKING — `listing_images`/`listing_tags`/`rating_aggregates`/`review_photos`/`collection_listings` had RLS ENABLED with ZERO policies~~ — ✅ RESOLVED 2026-07-01 (T01-FIX, Opus 4.8; PENDING security-review verdict).** Root cause: these were SPECCED in the ERD §3 RLS matrix (via-listing / via-review / read-public / via-collection) but their `CREATE POLICY` statements were OMITTED from `BETK_DATABASE_SCHEMA.sql`, so the verbatim Phase-01 migration left them RLS-enabled-no-policy → default-deny (the exact subset of T08 finding F1). **Fix = one additive migration `20260630232657_catalog_public_read_rls.sql`** adding 5 parent-scoped PERMISSIVE FOR SELECT policies: `listing_images_public`/`listing_tags_public` (EXISTS active+not-deleted listing), `review_photos_public` (EXISTS visible review), `collection_listings_public` (EXISTS live collection), `rating_aggregates_public` (`USING(TRUE)` — aggregate-only, no PII). Additive only (no table/column/grant change, no service-role, existing policies untouched). Applied to staging via MCP `apply_migration` (direct binary blocked — unlinked, no creds in env/chat; human-authorized; committed file is source-of-truth). Recorded in the remote ledger as version `20260630232657` — it **DOES** appear in `migration list`; local file renamed to match via `git mv` in R1 (2026-07-16), ledger↔local now aligned 1:1. `pg_policies`-verified (exactly 1 SELECT policy/table, intended predicates). Integration 8/8: positives surface (images/tags/rating/collection strip/visible review photos), negatives hold (draft + soft-deleted listing images, hidden review photos all → 0 rows for anon). Owner/admin child-row reads NOT added (belong to seller-listing-mgmt / admin-moderation phases; will OR-combine additively). T02/T04/T05/T06 public pages will now render images/ratings/tags/photos/collections.

### SMS delivery — BUILT + DEPLOYED; handset-delivery confirmation pending (pre-launch gate)
- **Production OTP delivery = TorvoSMS** (REST, POST /sms/send, `x-api-key` header) via Supabase Send SMS Hook → Edge Function `send-sms-hook`. **TorvoSMS REPLACES SMS Misr** (no longer the path). Provider shape: full-message-string — caller interpolates the OTP into the message body. Recipient format SETTLED: `countryCode:"20"` + **bare-national** `recipients:["1001124312"]` (TorvoSMS normalizes all forms to `201001124312`; format is not delivery-sensitive). `transformPhoneForTorvoSMS` TODO = RESOLVED.
- **T01a status = CODE COMPLETE + DEPLOYED + LIVE-INTEGRATED (2026-06-27, Opus).** Edge Function ACTIVE on staging (`verify_jwt=false`). Server chain proven green: GoTrue → signed hook → Standard-Webhooks signature verified → TorvoSMS accepted (`status: sent`) → `{}` 200 JSON → GoTrue "Hook ran successfully". Content-type fix applied: GoTrue rejects empty/`text/plain` hook responses (`hook_payload_invalid_content_type`) → success returns `{}` + `application/json`.
  - **OTP expiry 60s (R-A02 unchanged).** BETK Arabic copy matching 60s: `كود التحقق الخاص بك في بيتك هو {otp} — صالح لمدة ٦٠ ثانية`. Provider's "5 minutes" sample deliberately NOT used.
  - AC-AUTH-2 (verified by 11 unit tests): HMAC verify BEFORE parse (401 on fail); raw OTP / built message / API key / full body never logged, returned, persisted, or in Sentry; on error log only provider status/code + message id. API key + base URL + webhook secret in Edge Function secrets only.
- **Secret fix (2026-06-30):** `TORVOSMS_BASE_URL` was set to a wrong/trial endpoint → TorvoSMS returned `http_404` on every send → GoTrue returned 500 to app. Fixed: `TORVOSMS_BASE_URL` updated to the correct live host (`https://smsapi.torvochat.com`, host-only, no path suffix — function appends `/sms/send`). `TORVOSMS_SENDER_ID` also updated to the registered sender ID `3MS EGY`. Edge Function secrets updated via dashboard; Edge Function confirmed live: `{"fn":"send-sms-hook","level":"info","event":"sms_delivered"}`. **No code changes — purely a secrets fix.** Diagnosed via `scripts/dev/send-otp-test.ts` harness + Edge Function structured logs.
- **OPEN — handset delivery (provider-side, NOT code, NOT a T05 blocker):** TorvoSMS `sms_delivered` confirmed (2026-06-30). Pending confirmation: (a) SMS actually arrives on handset; (b) sender ID displays as `3MS EGY` on device. **HARD PRE-LAUNCH GATE:** real handset delivery must be confirmed before phone OTP ships to users.
- **DEV/TEST (Path A):** hook is LIVE; OTPs are visible in TorvoSMS dashboard log. Verify OTPs by reading the code from the dashboard log (deterministic). NOTE: enabling the hook disabled the Supabase Test-Phone-Number short-circuit — every send is a real (logged) TorvoSMS send. Test harness: `pnpm exec tsx scripts/dev/send-otp-test.ts <phone>`.
- **App code is delivery-agnostic:** `signInWithOtp()`/`verifyOtp()` are independent of delivery; any future provider/route change is dashboard/secrets only — zero app-code rework.

Auth provider configuration (Phone/OTP enable, Google OAuth enable, test phone numbers, OTP length = 6, OTP expiry = 60 s) is a **manual Supabase Dashboard action** — no MCP auth-config tool exists; no Supabase Management API token is present in this environment; the plain `supabase` CLI binary does not push hosted auth config. These settings have been confirmed applied by the human.

The 3 interval jobs (`expire-boosts`, `dispute-sla-alert`, `cleanup-otp-tokens`) are timezone-independent and were LEFT UNTOUCHED. Command bodies re-supplied verbatim; job names unchanged. Source parity: `BETK_DATABASE_SCHEMA.sql` cron section updated to the UTC schedules + dual-season comments; the original `20260622083154_cron.sql` was NOT edited. Applied via MCP `apply_migration` (CLI unusable — `config.toml` hook-secret validation fails locally); ledger↔local re-verified **18/18 1:1**. Live-verified via `cron.job` (3 new schedules + 3 unchanged; all `active=true`).

Live + integration-proven (6/6). The FROM-FILE-ONLY discipline held: it was flagged and deferred at T05/T14, not invented, then implemented against the ERD §7 / R-L05 / R-L06 spec.
- **T05 finding — grants not in source:** `0013_grants.sql` is standard Supabase role-grant boilerplate (step 057), the only non-verbatim file. Also: `betk`/`betk_analytics` must be added to `supabase/config.toml [api].schemas` before the Data API can reach them.
- **T05 finding — security advisors (hardening, for security gate):** ~21 tables are RLS-enabled-no-policy (default-deny, parent-scoped — remaining policies arrive in T08/later phases per C3 §5); `function_search_path_mutable` on the 6 functions; `extension_in_public` for pg_trgm/unaccent. All match the verbatim source; address during the 90-review-security gate.
- **T08 finding — orders/seller_profiles INSERT default-denied:** the OD-4 phone-gate on `orders` & `seller_profiles` is RESTRICTIVE-only with no PERMISSIVE ownership INSERT policy. Because a RESTRICTIVE policy can only subtract from what permissive policies allow, ALL authenticated inserts to these two tables are currently denied — phone or not. Phase 07 (checkout/orders) and Phase 04 (become-seller/seller_profiles) MUST add the permissive ownership INSERT policy so the phone-gate has something to restrict. `payouts` already has both (`payouts_insert` permissive + `payouts_phone_gate` restrictive) and is the working model; the harness proves the positive path there.
- **T08 finding — phone-gate denial not isolated on orders:** assertion 4 proves the positive path on `payouts` and a negative on `orders`, but the orders negative passes via default-deny (no permissive INSERT), not the gate itself. Optional hardening: add a payouts negative — service-role-seed a phone-NULL store owner, assert their `payouts` INSERT is rejected — to isolate the gate's denying effect from default-deny. Not blocking; track for the Phase 07 / 04 gate verification.
- **T08 finding — append-only tables are undeletable via the Data API:** `order_status_history` & `moderation_logs` carry `DO INSTEAD NOTHING` UPDATE/DELETE RULES, so rows inserted through PostgREST can never be torn down by the harness. Assertion 5 is therefore opt-in (`RLS_TEST_APPEND_ONLY=1`); by default it is recorded as a finding (enforcement is structurally guaranteed by the rules + absence of non-admin UPDATE/DELETE policies).
